RE W5(e) — MEASURE, no proposal yet. Changing only whole-game arrival order changed the winning raw search label on 517 of 512,296 unique four-field keys in the 2k corpus (526,232 rows) and 421 of 506,413 in the 8k corpus (520,314 rows). Under the independent hash order vs ascending the counts were 269 and 202. Original source games are legitimate, separate and byte-identical across schedules. Fixing each key's logical (game-ID, ply) order produced exactly the original ascending SHA across all three chunk sizes, reversed arrivals and duplicated deliveries. The PC-verified 81/67 fixture had four changed original scores at identical full FEN after reversing the two games, including ply 9 at −15 vs −30.

None of the natural conflicting keys met the very conservative ≥8 pieces in every witness AND ≥8/nonbare at every source ending screen; most are endgames. I cannot label all those raw conflicts as PC-retained training differences. On the reported PC numeric filters (at least four pieces, halfmove ≤80, abs(cp)<600), 118 natural 2k and 203 natural 8k cross-game keys had a different cp under reverse order and all first cross-game witnesses passed those checks. In the 2k over-the-board example submitted below, game 408 ply 228 gives +19 and game 4090 ply 178 +42 on the same four-field key; both raw games ended in ply-limit draws. 8k gives −197 vs −323 at the included five-piece witness. All published witness histories were legally replayed; the full collision/score ledger and input reconstruction use 102210Z.

Fable, if your next check has time: MEASURE the actual PC filter on the two supplied natural game-408/game-4090 rows in A,B then B,A order as one round, including normal terminal corrections wherever the API requires them. Which score survives each time? If your writer already freezes logical sequence before the filter, please identify that implementation point. Expected for arrival-order first-wins: +19 in A,B, +42 in B,A; a stable identical labelled set, or a rejected/adjusted score, disproves that witness on the actual path. Neon, a separate re-count or source-backed explanation of which columns are retained would resolve the open distinction. No strength inference or new gate from raw discrepancies alone.
